        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Replace the preview panel in the video editor top pane with a shots browser panel (on all pages except the main video travel tool page).

## What to build

A horizontal-scrolling shots panel in the top pane of the video editor that:

1. Shows all project shots in a compact grid (4-5 per row, tightly packed)
2. Each shot shows its thumbnail/preview (final video thumbnail if available, otherwise first image)
3. Horizontal scroll to browse all shots
4. Search/filter by shot name
5. Shots are draggable onto the timeline (already have shot drag type + SortableShotItem drag support)
6. When a clip is selected on the timeline, the corresponding shot is highlighted in the panel
7. Can filter to show only shots related to the current selection
8. When dropped on timeline: if shot has a final video → show video, otherwise → show images

## Existing components to reuse
- ShotListDisplay (src/tools/travel-between-images/components/VideoGallery/ShotListDisplay.tsx) — main shot grid
- SortableShotItem (src/tools/travel-between-images/components/VideoGallery/SortableShotItem.tsx) — draggable shot card with shot drag data
- VideoShotDisplay / VideoShotDisplayParts — shot card rendering
- useShotFinalVideos — final video data
- useShots/ShotsContext — shot data
- The shot drag type already exists in shared DnD (setShotDragData/getShotDropData)

## Layout
- The top pane currently shows PreviewPanel (Remotion preview)
- On pages OTHER than the main video travel tool page, replace this with the shots panel
- Keep it compact — this is a browse/drag-source panel, not a full editor
- Consider a toggle to switch between preview and shots panel

## Highlighting/filtering
- selectedClipIds → meta → assetGenerationMap → generationIds → find shots containing those generations
- Highlight matching shots with a ring/border treatment
- Optional filter toggle: 'Show related shots only'

## Shot drop behavior (already mostly built)
- useExternalDrop already handles shot drops
- It checks finalVideoMap — if shot has video, creates video clip; otherwise creates image clips
- Creates a pinned group automatically

## Key files to modify
- src/tools/video-editor/components/VideoEditorShell.tsx — layout, conditionally show shots panel vs preview
- New: src/tools/video-editor/components/ShotsPanel.tsx (or similar) — the compact shots browser
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineEditor.tsx — expose selectedShotIds for highlighting
- May need a compact variant of ShotListDisplay or a new simplified component

User notes and answers:
- Addressing key flags: The shots panel replaces the PreviewPanel in the TOP PANE of VideoEditorShell. It does NOT go in the properties/side panel. The layout has 3 areas: top (preview), right (properties), bottom (timeline). The shots panel goes in the top area. It needs to be a new component that lives in the video-editor tool folder. For highlighting: expose selectedGenerationIds from TimelineEditor via context so the shots panel can read it. For the compact layout: create a new ShotsPanelCard component (simpler than VideoShotDisplay) that shows just thumbnail + name + drag handle. The panel should have its own DndContext for reordering if needed, but primarily serves as a drag SOURCE for dropping onto the timeline.
- Additional layout requirements: The shots panel should be resizable like the bottom timeline pane. When both the shots panel (top) and the generation/properties pane (right) are locked/visible, they should each take 50% of the available space. The shots panel height should be adjustable with a drag handle, same pattern as the timeline height adjuster.

(\n+        <div className=\"flex flex-1 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-dashed text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n+          Failed to load shots\n+        </div>\n+      ) : filteredShots.length === 0 ? (\n+        <div className=\"flex flex-1 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-dashed text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n+          {(shots?.length ?? 0) === 0 ? 'No shots yet' : 'No matching shots'}\n+        </div>\n+      ) : (\n+        <div className=\"min-h-0 overflow-x-auto overflow-y-hidden\">\n+          <div className=\"grid auto-cols-[11rem] grid-flow-col grid-rows-2 gap-3 pb-2\">\n+            {filteredShots.map((shot) => (\n+              <ShotsPanelCard\n+                key={shot.id}\n+                shot={shot}\n+                thumbnailUrl={getShotThumbnail(shot, finalVideoMap.get(shot.id)?.thumbnailUrl)}\n+                highlighted={highlightedShotIds.has(shot.id)}\n+              />\n+            ))}\n+          </div>\n+        </div>\n+      )}\n+    </div>\n+  );\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Replace the preview panel in the video editor top pane with a shots browser panel (on all pages except the main video travel tool page).

## What to build

A horizontal-scrolling shots panel in the top pane of the video editor that:

1. Shows all project shots in a compact grid (4-5 per row, tightly packed)
2. Each shot shows its thumbnail/preview (final video thumbnail if available, otherwise first image)
3. Horizontal scroll to browse all shots
4. Search/filter by shot name
5. Shots are draggable onto the timeline (already have shot drag type + SortableShotItem drag support)
6. When a clip is selected on the timeline, the corresponding shot is highlighted in the panel
7. Can filter to show only shots related to the current selection
8. When dropped on timeline: if shot has a final video → show video, otherwise → show images

## Existing components to reuse
- ShotListDisplay (src/tools/travel-between-images/components/VideoGallery/ShotListDisplay.tsx) — main shot grid
- SortableShotItem (src/tools/travel-between-images/components/VideoGallery/SortableShotItem.tsx) — draggable shot card with shot drag data
- VideoShotDisplay / VideoShotDisplayParts — shot card rendering
- useShotFinalVideos — final video data
- useShots/ShotsContext — shot data
- The shot drag type already exists in shared DnD (setShotDragData/getShotDropData)

## Layout
- The top pane currently shows PreviewPanel (Remotion preview)
- On pages OTHER than the main video travel tool page, replace this with the shots panel
- Keep it compact — this is a browse/drag-source panel, not a full editor
- Consider a toggle to switch between preview and shots panel

## Highlighting/filtering
- selectedClipIds → meta → assetGenerationMap → generationIds → find shots containing those generations
- Highlight matching shots with a ring/border treatment
- Optional filter toggle: 'Show related shots only'

## Shot drop behavior (already mostly built)
- useExternalDrop already handles shot drops
- It checks finalVideoMap — if shot has video, creates video clip; otherwise creates image clips
- Creates a pinned group automatically

## Key files to modify
- src/tools/video-editor/components/VideoEditorShell.tsx — layout, conditionally show shots panel vs preview
- New: src/tools/video-editor/components/ShotsPanel.tsx (or similar) — the compact shots browser
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineEditor.tsx — expose selectedShotIds for highlighting
- May need a compact variant of ShotListDisplay or a new simplified component

User notes and answers:
- Addressing key flags: The shots panel replaces the PreviewPanel in the TOP PANE of VideoEditorShell. It does NOT go in the properties/side panel. The layout has 3 areas: top (preview), right (properties), bottom (timeline). The shots panel goes in the top area. It needs to be a new component that lives in the video-editor tool folder. For highlighting: expose selectedGenerationIds from TimelineEditor via context so the shots panel can read it. For the compact layout: create a new ShotsPanelCard component (simpler than VideoShotDisplay) that shows just thumbnail + name + drag handle. The panel should have its own DndContext for reordering if needed, but primarily serves as a drag SOURCE for dropping onto the timeline.
- Additional layout requirements: The shots panel should be resizable like the bottom timeline pane. When both the shots panel (top) and the generation/properties pane (right) are locked/visible, they should each take 50% of the available space. The shots panel height should be adjustable with a drag handle, same pattern as the timeline height adjuster.

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Replace the preview panel in the video editor top pane with a shots browser panel (on all pages except the main video travel tool page).

## What to build

A horizontal-scrolling shots panel in the top pane of the video editor that:

1. Shows all project shots in a compact grid (4-5 per row, tightly packed)
2. Each shot shows its thumbnail/preview (final video thumbnail if available, otherwise first image)
3. Horizontal scroll to browse all shots
4. Search/filter by shot name
5. Shots are draggable onto the timeline (already have shot drag type + SortableShotItem drag support)
6. When a clip is selected on the timeline, the corresponding shot is highlighted in the panel
7. Can filter to show only shots related to the current selection
8. When dropped on timeline: if shot has a final video → show video, otherwise → show images

## Existing components to reuse
- ShotListDisplay (src/tools/travel-between-images/components/VideoGallery/ShotListDisplay.tsx) — main shot grid
- SortableShotItem (src/tools/travel-between-images/components/VideoGallery/SortableShotItem.tsx) — draggable shot card with shot drag data
- VideoShotDisplay / VideoShotDisplayParts — shot card rendering
- useShotFinalVideos — final video data
- useShots/ShotsContext — shot data
- The shot drag type already exists in shared DnD (setShotDragData/getShotDropData)

## Layout
- The top pane currently shows PreviewPanel (Remotion preview)
- On pages OTHER than the main video travel tool page, replace this with the shots panel
- Keep it compact — this is a browse/drag-source panel, not a full editor
- Consider a toggle to switch between preview and shots panel

## Highlighting/filtering
- selectedClipIds → meta → assetGenerationMap → generationIds → find shots containing those generations
- Highlight matching shots with a ring/border treatment
- Optional filter toggle: 'Show related shots only'

## Shot drop behavior (already mostly built)
- useExternalDrop already handles shot drops
- It checks finalVideoMap — if shot has video, creates video clip; otherwise creates image clips
- Creates a pinned group automatically

## Key files to modify
- src/tools/video-editor/components/VideoEditorShell.tsx — layout, conditionally show shots panel vs preview
- New: src/tools/video-editor/components/ShotsPanel.tsx (or similar) — the compact shots browser
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineEditor.tsx — expose selectedShotIds for highlighting
- May need a compact variant of ShotListDisplay or a new simplified component

User notes and answers:
- Addressing key flags: The shots panel replaces the PreviewPanel in the TOP PANE of VideoEditorShell. It does NOT go in the properties/side panel. The layout has 3 areas: top (preview), right (properties), bottom (timeline). The shots panel goes in the top area. It needs to be a new component that lives in the video-editor tool folder. For highlighting: expose selectedGenerationIds from TimelineEditor via context so the shots panel can read it. For the compact layout: create a new ShotsPanelCard component (simpler than VideoShotDisplay) that shows just thumbnail + name + drag handle. The panel should have its own DndContext for reordering if needed, but primarily serves as a drag SOURCE for dropping onto the timeline.
- Additional layout requirements: The shots panel should be resizable like the bottom timeline pane. When both the shots panel (top) and the generation/properties pane (right) are locked/visible, they should each take 50% of the available space. The shots panel height should be adjustable with a drag handle, same pattern as the timeline height adjuster.

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 2 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content.
